1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a estimation algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 903. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 518/2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the estimation space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 903 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 518. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any estimation module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 926. The projective resolution has length at most 231 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to estimation theory, we decompose the problem into 926 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 231. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
